## Families

- **Room to connect** (`connect`): free grid capacity, hosting capacity or connection queues per substation, node or area (Grid capacity layer, site analysis). have 6, built 21, not published 14, blocked 0.
- **Every generating unit** (`units`): national registers of generating units (layer per country under Power plants). have 2, built 8, not published 19, blocked 0.
- **Live mix and CO₂** (`live_mix`): generation by source at the operator's own time step, and CO₂ per kWh where the operator publishes it (country cards). have 23, built 4, not published 2, blocked 0.
- **Redispatch and curtailment** (`congestion`): redispatch, countertrading, congestion-management costs and curtailment (country cards, agent catalogue). have 0, built 15, not published 14, blocked 0.
- **Wholesale prices** (`prices`): day-ahead and imbalance (or real-time) prices (Prices views, imbalance card). have 24, built 3, not published 2, blocked 0.
- **What consumers pay** (`retail`): household and business electricity and gas prices (retail colouring, country report). have 28, built 1, not published 0, blocked 0.
- **Emitters per installation** (`emitters`): verified CO₂ per installation (CO₂ per installation layer). have 28, built 1, not published 0, blocked 0.
- **Plants offline now** (`offline`): generating units out of service now (planned and forced). have 26, built 1, not published 2, blocked 0.
- **EV charging** (`ev`): public charge points (EV charging layer) and counts per country. have 1, built 28, not published 0, blocked 0.
- **Gas** (`gas`): gas flows, storage, LNG and prices. have 26, built 1, not published 2, blocked 0.
- **Reliability** (`reliability`): interruptions per customer (SAIDI, SAIFI) or outages per area. have 1, built 28, not published 0, blocked 0.
- **Outlines at every level** (`outlines`): municipality, neighbourhood and small-area outlines for areas, choropleths and resolve_area (brief 27 B1); EU LAU left out (GISCO: non-commercial only). have 0, built 29, not published 0, blocked 0.
- **City outlines (GHS urban centres)** (`urban_centres`): GHS Urban Centre Database R2024: city outlines worldwide with their 2025 population. have 0, built 29, not published 0, blocked 0.
- **Rasters summed in any area** (`rasters`): people and built-up surface (GHSL), land cover (WorldCover), slope (Copernicus DEM), solar and wind resource, river flood depth, read by area_stats. have 0, built 29, not published 0, blocked 0.
- **Energy consumption per small area** (`consumption`): electricity and gas use per municipality or smaller area, MWh a year (or averages as published), with the level named. have 0, built 10, not published 19, blocked 0.
- **Heating fuel per small area** (`heating`): how homes are heated per small area, as published (counts or shares). have 0, built 7, not published 21, blocked 1.
- **Energy poverty** (`energy_poverty`): households unable to keep warm, fuel poverty or energy burden, at the finest level published. have 0, built 29, not published 0, blocked 0.
- **Industrial facilities and releases** (`industrial`): every reporting facility with its position and releases to air and water; large combustion plants' fuel input. have 0, built 29, not published 0, blocked 0.
- **Oil and gas facilities** (`oil_gas_facilities`): refineries, processing plants, storage and terminals, pipelines. have 0, built 29, not published 0, blocked 0.
- **CO₂ storage** (`co2_storage`): geological storage units and their capacity. have 0, built 27, not published 1, blocked 1.
- **Heat demand** (`heat_demand`): heat demand density (100 m) and industrial excess heat sites. have 0, built 28, not published 1, blocked 0.
- **Fires** (`fires`): active fire detections and burnt areas near the grid. have 0, built 1, not published 0, blocked 28.
- **Flood zones** (`flood_zones`): river flood hazard, and national flood zones where published. have 28, built 1, not published 0, blocked 0.
- **Wetlands** (`wetlands`): wetlands as siting constraints. have 0, built 0, not published 0, blocked 29.
- **Dams and reservoirs** (`dams`): barriers and reservoirs (Global Dam Watch). have 0, built 29, not published 0, blocked 0.
- **Who owns the assets** (`ownership`): owners and ownership chains of GEM-tracked plants, mines and pipelines. have 0, built 29, not published 0, blocked 0.
- **Renewables acceleration and zoning areas** (`acceleration_areas`): areas designated for renewables (RED III Art. 15c. have 0, built 0, not published 29, blocked 0.
- **Agricultural land quality** (`farmland_quality`): agricultural land classification or prime farmland. have 0, built 1, not published 28, blocked 0.
- **Brownfield and contaminated land** (`brownfield`): brownfield registers and polluted sites. have 0, built 1, not published 28, blocked 0.
- **Land prices** (`land_prices`): agricultural land prices and rents. have 0, built 27, not published 2, blocked 0.
- **Heritage and aviation constraints** (`heritage_aviation`): listed heritage. have 0, built 29, not published 0, blocked 0.
- **Major energy consents** (`energy_consents`): consents and permits for large energy projects. have 0, built 0, not published 28, blocked 1.
- **Planned grid** (`planned_grid`): grid projects of the national network plan. have 28, built 0, not published 0, blocked 1.
- **Locational network charges** (`network_charges`): transmission or distribution charges per zone or utility. have 0, built 29, not published 0, blocked 0.
- **Large loads and data-centre demand** (`large_load`): large-load connection queues or data-centre consumption. have 28, built 1, not published 0, blocked 0.
- **Distribution network data** (`distribution_data`): DSO open data (embedded capacity. have 0, built 1, not published 28, blocked 0.
- **Balancing and reserves** (`balancing`): reserve and balancing prices and volumes. have 0, built 2, not published 21, blocked 6.
- **Capacity markets** (`capacity_markets`): capacity auction results and registers. have 0, built 0, not published 29, blocked 0.
- **Support contracts** (`support_contracts`): contracts for difference and auction awards per project. have 0, built 0, not published 28, blocked 1.

- **Drought** (`drought`): drought indicators or drought monitor polygons. have 28, built 1, not published 0, blocked 0.
- **Wildfire hazard** (`wildfire_hazard`): wildfire hazard or danger. have 1, built 0, not published 28, blocked 0.
- **Coastal flood and sea level** (`coastal_flood`): coastal flood hazard and sea-level rise inundation. have 0, built 0, not published 29, blocked 0.
- **Landslides** (`landslides`): landslide inventory or susceptibility. have 0, built 0, not published 29, blocked 0.
- **Storms** (`storms`): storm events with position and damage. have 0, built 1, not published 28, blocked 0.
- **Geomagnetic hazard** (`geomagnetic`): geoelectric hazard to transformers. have 0, built 0, not published 29, blocked 0.
- **Ground motion and subsidence** (`subsidence`): ground motion (InSAR). have 0, built 0, not published 1, blocked 28.
- **EV registrations per area** (`ev_registrations`): electric vehicles registered per area. have 0, built 28, not published 1, blocked 0.
- **Heat pumps** (`heat_pumps`): heat pumps installed per area. have 0, built 27, not published 2, blocked 0.
- **Rooftop and small solar** (`rooftop_solar`): small solar installations per area. have 0, built 27, not published 1, blocked 1.
- **Building energy certificates** (`building_certificates`): energy performance certificates (aggregates per area). have 0, built 1, not published 28, blocked 0.
- **Data-centre electricity** (`data_centre_demand`): electricity used by data centres. have 0, built 1, not published 28, blocked 0.
- **Degree days** (`degree_days`): heating and cooling degree days. have 0, built 27, not published 2, blocked 0.
- **Water depth** (`bathymetry`): bathymetry grid. have 0, built 0, not published 29, blocked 0.
- **Shipping density** (`shipping_density`): vessel density or AIS transit counts. have 0, built 0, not published 29, blocked 0.

## Cells that are not on the map

- Room to connect · BE · not_published: Elia and Sibelga answer HTTP 403 behind Cloudflare's check (2026-10-27); Elia's open-data portal holds no hosting-capacity set; Fluvius and ORES pages answer 404. Browser discovery in Actions (2026-10-28, an honest Chromium) did not pass the check. (https://www.elia.be/en/customers/connection/grid-hosting-capacity, checked 2026-10-28)
- Room to connect · CY · not_published: TSO Cyprus answers HTTP 403 behind Cloudflare; EAC's hosting-capacity page resets the connection (2026-10-27). Browser discovery in Actions (2026-10-28, an honest Chromium) did not pass the check. (https://www.eac.com.cy/EN/RegulatedActivities/Distribution/renewableenergy/Pages/hosting-capacity.aspx, checked 2026-10-28)
- Room to connect · FI · built: Fingrid's Verkkokiikari is a map without a download; Caruna publishes no capacity map; four small DSOs (Rejlers-hosted maps: K-Sat, Nivos, Naantalin Energia, Lehtimäki) publish MVA bands without a side, not harmonised (2026-10-28) (https://www.elenia.fi/en/electricity-consumption-and-production/green-transition/map-of-available-capacity, checked 2026-10-28)
- Room to connect · DE · built: TenneT DE and the E.ON DSOs (Westnetz, E.DIS, Bayernwerk, Avacon) answer HTTP 403 behind Cloudflare's check; Netze BW and VNBdigital publish no capacity file; the §14e platform lists services only (https://www.50hertz.com/de/Netz/Netzanschluss, checked 2026-10-27)
- Room to connect · GR · built: IPTO (ADMIE) answers HTTP 403 / 404 for its connection pages (https://apps.deddie.gr/WebAPE/main.html, checked 2026-10-27)
- Room to connect · LV · not_published: AST answers HTTP 403 behind Cloudflare; Sadales tīkls' free-capacity map loads from an obfuscated host and answers HTTP 451 to a script that names itself (not bypassed). Browser discovery in Actions (2026-10-28, an honest Chromium) did not pass the check. (https://karte.sadalestikls.lv/lv/briva-jauda, checked 2026-10-28)
- Room to connect · RO · not_published: Transelectrica (browser discovery passed, 2026-10-28) publishes only the requested capacity per grid zone A-J as an image and says the available capacity per zone is on its auction platform (data request); Delgaz and E-Distribuție: the browser did not pass the check; Distribuție Energie Electrică publishes no capacity file; Rețele Electrice 403 (https://www.transelectrica.ro/web/tel/racordare, checked 2026-10-28)
- Room to connect · SK · built: SEPS publishes a Power BI report only; SSD resets the connection (2026-10-27) (https://www.zsdis.sk/Uvod/Online-sluzby/Mapa-pripojitelnosti, checked 2026-10-27)
- Room to connect · ES · built: i-DE answers HTTP 503 and UFD HTTP 403 to scripts (2026-10-27) (https://redes-spain.opendatasoft.com, checked 2026-10-27)
- Room to connect · GB · built: ENWL, SPEN and UKPN wait for their keys (ENWL_API_KEY, SPEN_API_KEY, UKPN_API_KEY) (https://connecteddata.nationalgrid.co.uk/dataset/ltds-capacity-heatmap, checked 2026-10-28)
- Room to connect · US · built: ConEd, National Grid NY/MA, Xcel, Duke and Dominion publish hosting capacity per feeder line or grid cell only (not nodes); MISO and SPP headroom tools are interactive (https://drpep.sce.com, checked 2026-10-28)
- Room to connect · NO · not_published: Statnett publishes connection requests and reservations as Power BI reports only (no export); Elvia and NVE publish no capacity map or file (https://www.statnett.no/nettkapasitet-til-produksjon-og-forbruk/foresporsler-og-reservasjon-i-nettet/, checked 2026-10-28)
- Room to connect · CH · built: positions by OpenStreetMap name; DSOs (EKZ, BKW, CKW, Groupe E, Romande Energie, IWB) publish no capacity file; ElCom none (https://www.swissgrid.ch/en/home/customers/topics/grid-connection.html, checked 2026-10-28)
- Room to connect · IS · not_published: Landsnet and RARIK publish no capacity map or file (https://www.landsnet.is/, checked 2026-10-28)
- Room to connect · RS · not_published: EMS publishes cross-border capacity only; Elektrodistribucija Srbije no capacity file (https://ems.rs/opis-i-kapaciteti/, checked 2026-10-28)
- Room to connect · BA · not_published: NOS BiH timed out; Elektroprenos BiH publishes no capacity file (https://www.elprenos.ba/, checked 2026-10-28)

- Room to connect · MK · not_published: MEPSO connection page and Elektrodistribucija procedures only; no capacity file (https://www.mepso.com.mk/index.php/mk/doma/za-nas/prikluchoci, checked 2026-10-28)
- Room to connect · AL · not_published: OST answers a Cloudflare challenge (403); OSHEE describes new connections only. Browser discovery in Actions (2026-10-28, an honest Chromium) did not pass the check. (https://www.ost.al/, checked 2026-10-28)

- Live mix and CO₂ · NL · have: NED (Nationaal Energie Dashboard) needs a free key: NED_API_KEY (https://ned.nl) (https://transparency.entsoe.eu, checked 2026-10-19)

- Redispatch and curtailment · BG · not_published: ENTSO-E answered 'No matching data' for internal and cross-border redispatch, countertrading and congestion costs over 400 days (2025-09-15 to 2026-10-20, every month asked); no national series found as data (https://transparency.entsoe.eu, checked 2026-10-20)

- Emitters per installation · GB · built: Emissions per installation; the report publishes no position or activity, so GB installations are listed but not on the map (https://reports.view-emissions-trading-registry.service.gov.uk/ets-reports.html, checked 2026-10-20)
- Plants offline now · CY · not_published: ENTSO-E A80 for Cyprus (10YCY-1001A0003J) answered no outage document (wave7 job H, 2026-10-20); TSO Cyprus and EAC publish no list of units offline (https://transparency.entsoe.eu, checked 2026-10-20)
- Plants offline now · IE · have: The SEM zone (Ireland and Northern Ireland) is queried since wave 7; it reported no unit offline at the first read (2026-10-20 03:55 UTC), listed as 0 MW (https://transparency.entsoe.eu, checked 2026-10-20)
- Plants offline now · LU · have: Luxembourg shares the DE-LU bidding zone; its units (Vianden) report there and are counted under Germany (https://transparency.entsoe.eu, checked 2026-10-20)
- Plants offline now · MT · not_published: ENTSO-E A80 for Malta (10Y1001A1001A93C) answered no outage document (wave7 job H, 2026-10-20); Enemalta publishes no list of units offline (https://transparency.entsoe.eu, checked 2026-10-20)
- Plants offline now · US · have: US markets publish no unit outages; fossil units at zero output hour by hour (https://campd.epa.gov, checked 2026-10-15)
